    36	/**
    37	 * @title CC48RegistryTimelockGovernance
    38	 * @notice CC-48 round-7, from the archived original security checklist:
    39	 *
    40	 *           "proxy 升级新 cap slot 初值为 0：UpgradeRegistryTo580 已把
    41	 *            setCreditPolicy(perProposal,total) 放入同一批计划；round 7
    42	 *            必须补一个真实 Timelock schedule/execute 测试，证明 upgrade + baseline/caps
    43	 *            不存在中间可执行窗口。"
    44	 *           "setCreditPolicy 仍是 immediate onlyOwner ... 若是 Timelock，部署/迁移门禁和
    45	 *            测试必须验证 getMinDelay>0 及 owner 实际等于该 Timelock；不得只打印 calldata。"
    46	 *
    47	 *         Everything here runs against a REAL `TimelockController` (OpenZeppelin v5.0.2)
    48	 *         owning a REAL `ERC1967Proxy` Registry — no mock of the governance path, because
    49	 *         the property being asserted IS the governance path. The previous evidence for
    50	 *         this was `UpgradeRegistryTo580` printing `scheduleBatch` calldata, which proves
    51	 *         nothing about whether the operation can be executed, executed in pieces, or
    52	 *         executed at all.
    53	 *
    54	 *         CC-48 round-8 LOW-5 — WHAT "BINDS TO THE SHIPPED PARAMETERS" NOW MEANS. Round 7
    55	 *         claimed that here while holding its OWN `BATCH_SALT` constant and its OWN
    56	 *         hand-written copy of the batch, so changing the salt in `UpgradeRegistryTo580`
    57	 *         left this test green. That claim was false and is retracted. Salt, predecessor
    58	 *         and every payload now comes from `RegistryUpgradeBatchLib`, which the SCRIPT
    59	 *         also calls — there is one definition in the repository, so an edit to the shipped
    60	 *         batch is an edit to what this test asserts, by construction rather than by
    61	 *         discipline.
    62	 */
    63	contract CC48RegistryTimelockGovernance is Test {
    64	    /// @dev NOT re-declared here: aliased straight to the shipped definition, so there is
    65	    ///      no second value that could drift from the script's.
    66	    bytes32 internal constant BATCH_SALT = RegistryUpgradeBatchLib.BATCH_SALT;
    67	    bytes32 internal constant NO_PREDECESSOR = RegistryUpgradeBatchLib.NO_PREDECESSOR;
    68	
    69	    uint256 internal constant MIN_DELAY = 2 days;
    70	
    71	    uint256 internal constant PER_PROPOSAL_CAP = 600 ether;
    72	    uint256 internal constant TOTAL_CAP = 200_000 ether;
    73	    /// @dev CC-48 round-9: the batch no longer carries an operator-supplied aPNT baseline.
    74	    ///      It carries the POPULATION, and the contract derives the stock from its own
    75	    ///      `globalReputation` storage. `_seedUsers()` returns that membership list.
    76	    address internal constant SEED_USER_A = address(0xA11CE);
    77	    address internal constant SEED_USER_B = address(0xB0B);
    78	
    79	    Registry internal registry;
    80	    TimelockMockStaking internal staking;
    81	    TimelockController internal timelock;
    82	    address internal newAggregator;
    83	    address internal oldAggregator;
    84	
    85	    address internal proposer = address(0x9401);
    86	    address internal executor = address(0x9402);
    87	    address internal stranger = address(0xBAD);
    88	
    89	    function setUp() public {
    90	        // A non-zero staking address is required, not incidental: step (3) of the batch
    91	        // authorises the new aggregator as a slasher ON GTokenStaking, so a proxy wired to
    92	        // address(0) would produce a batch whose third call goes nowhere.
    93	        staking = new TimelockMockStaking(address(this));
    94	        registry = UUPSDeployHelper.deployRegistryProxy(
    95	            address(this), address(staking), address(new TimelockMockSBT())
    96	        );
    97	        newAggregator = address(new TimelockMockBLS());
    98	        // Every real upgrade rotates AWAY from something, and that predecessor keeps its
    99	        // slasher authorisation unless the batch revokes it. Modelling it as a distinct
   100	        // address is what lets the shape assertions below see the revoke step at all.
   101	        oldAggregator = address(new TimelockMockBLS());
   102	
   103	        // Model the state an UPGRADED proxy is actually in. `initialize` seeds
   104	        // `maxTotalCreditExposure`, but `initialize` does not run on an upgrade: a proxy
   105	        // coming from a build that predates these slots reads 0 for both, which is exactly
   106	        // the window step (3) of the batch exists to close. Writing them to 0 through the
   107	        // contract's own setter models that without hard-coding a storage slot index.
   108	        registry.setCreditPolicy(0, 0);
   109	        assertEq(registry.maxTotalCreditExposure(), 0, "pre-batch cap must read 0");
   110	
   111	        address[] memory proposers = new address[](1);
   112	        proposers[0] = proposer;
   113	        address[] memory executors = new address[](1);
   114	        executors[0] = executor;
   115	        // admin = address(0): nobody can bypass the delay by re-granting roles later.
   116	        timelock = new TimelockController(MIN_DELAY, proposers, executors, address(0));
   117	
   118	        // CC-48 round-10 (adversarial review): the predecessor is ARMED here, while this
   119	        // test contract still owns the stand-in. Without this the fixture's own claim --
   120	        // "that predecessor keeps its slasher authorisation unless the batch revokes it" --
   121	        // was never modelled: `authorizedSlashers[oldAggregator]` started false, so the
   122	        // post-batch `assertFalse` held vacuously and a batch (or a mock) that ignored the
   123	        // revoke entirely still passed. Arming it first is what turns that assertion into
   124	        // a statement about the revoke.
   125	        staking.setAuthorizedSlasher(oldAggregator, true);
   126	        assertTrue(staking.authorizedSlashers(oldAggregator), "predecessor starts ARMED, as on a live chain");
   127	
   128	        registry.transferOwnership(address(timelock));
   129	        // D5b GOV-2: Registry ownership is two-step; the Timelock accepts (modelled by a prank —
   130	        // on chain this is the scheduled `acceptOwnership`, see runbook M1).
   131	        vm.prank(address(timelock));
   132	        registry.acceptOwnership();
   133	        // Both subjects answer to ONE principal — the deployment shape `UpgradeRegistryTo580`
   134	        // asserts (`IOwned(staking).owner() == IOwned(proxy).owner()`) before it emits the
   135	        // batch. Steps (3) and (4) are `onlyOwner` on the real GTokenStaking, so without
   136	        // this the operation could not carry them and the atomicity property under test
   137	        // would not be available in the first place.
   138	        staking.transferOwnership(address(timelock));
   139	    }
   140	
   141	    // =================================================================
   142	    // The two properties the checklist asks the GATE to assert
   143	    // =================================================================
   144	
   145	    /// `Registry.setCreditPolicy` is immediate `onlyOwner`, so "Registry is behind a
   146	    /// Timelock" is only true if the Timelock is BOTH the owner AND actually delaying.
   147	    /// `UpgradeRegistryTo580` now asserts exactly this pair when `TIMELOCK` is set.
   148	    function test_GateAssertsOwnershipAndANonZeroMinDelay() public view {
   149	        assertEq(registry.owner(), address(timelock), "owner is the Timelock");
   150	        assertGt(timelock.getMinDelay(), 0, "and the Timelock actually delays");
   151	
   152	        // The shipped gate, run against the real pair — not a restatement of it.
   153	        GovernanceOwnerGate.requireTimelockOwner(
   154	            address(registry), registry.owner(), address(timelock), "Registry"
   155	        );
   156	    }
   157	
   158	    /// A Timelock is not an M-of-N owner and the aggregator gate must keep refusing it,
   159	    /// even though Registry may legitimately be owned by one.
   160	    function test_ATimelockIsNotAcceptedWhereMofNIsRequired() public view {
   161	        (bool ok,, string memory why) = GovernanceOwnerGate.readMofN(address(timelock));
   162	        assertFalse(ok, "TimelockController has no getThreshold()");
   163	        assertTrue(bytes(why).length > 0);
   164	    }
   165	
   166	    // =================================================================
   167	    // The batch: scheduled once, executed once, every step or none
   168	    // =================================================================
   169	
   170	    function test_UpgradeAndCapsLandInOneTransactionWithNoIntermediateWindow() public {
   171	        (address[] memory targets, uint256[] memory values, bytes[] memory payloads) = _batch();
   172	
   173	        // The batch under test IS the shipped shape. Every call addresses the proxy EXCEPT
   174	        // the slasher authorisation, which must target GTokenStaking — a batch reaching any
   175	        // other address would not be the operation `UpgradeRegistryTo580` schedules, and
   176	        // everything below would be asserting about something else. Pinning the exception by
   177	        // index rather than allowing "proxy or staking" anywhere keeps the ordering asserted
   178	        // too: the authorisation has to be step (3), inside the same atomic operation.
   179	        // CC-48 round-10 LOW: the length is a LITERAL here, not `RegistryUpgradeBatchLib.
   180	        // BATCH_LENGTH`. `buildBatch` sizes the array FROM that constant, so asserting the
   181	        // constant is a tautology that no shrink of the batch can fail. Writing six by hand
   182	        // means a batch that loses a step has to be typed twice before it is green.
   183	        assertEq(targets.length, 6, "six calls: five plus the predecessor disarm");
   184	        address stakingTarget = address(registry.GTOKEN_STAKING());
   185	        assertTrue(stakingTarget != address(0), "staking must be wired for the batch to be meaningful");
   186	        for (uint256 i = 0; i < targets.length; ++i) {
   187	            if (i == 2 || i == 3) {
   188	                assertEq(targets[i], stakingTarget, "steps 3 and 4 are the slasher grant and revoke");
   189	            } else {
   190	                assertEq(targets[i], address(registry), "every other call targets the Registry proxy");
   191	            }
   192	            assertEq(values[i], 0, "no ether moves");
   193	        }
   194	        // CC-48 round-10 MEDIUM-1: the loop above pins only the TARGET of steps (3) and
   195	        // (4), and a target cannot tell a grant from a revoke -- both are
   196	        // `setAuthorizedSlasher` on the same GTokenStaking address. Mutating step (4) from
   197	        // (oldAggregator, false) to (oldAggregator, true), or to (newAggregator, false),
   198	        // left the whole suite green. The second of those is exactly the failure the
   199	        // revoke step was written not to cause: disarming, inside the same atomic batch,
   200	        // the aggregator that step (3) armed two calls earlier -- the reason this commit
   201	        // exists at all. Pin the payload by value so both mutations are red.
   202	        //
   203	        // Step (3) is pinned here TOO. An earlier version of this comment argued it was
   204	        // redundant because `test_SameAggregatorIsGrantedAndNotRevoked` already asserts
   205	        // that payload by value -- but that test only ever builds the `old == new` branch,
   206	        // and `test_FirstDeploymentBatchOmitsTheRevokeStep` only counts SELECTORS, so
   207	        // `(newAggregator, false)` still reads as one slasher call there. An
   208	        // input-dependent mutation -- emitting `false` only when `oldAggregator` is
   209	        // `address(0)`, say -- slips past both. "Unconditional line" was an argument about
   210	        // the code as written, not about the code as it might be edited, which is the only
   211	        // thing a test can defend.
   212	        assertEq(
   213	            keccak256(payloads[2]),
   214	            keccak256(abi.encodeCall(IStakingSlasherAuth.setAuthorizedSlasher, (newAggregator, true))),
   215	            "step (3) ARMS the new aggregator"
   216	        );
   217	        assertEq(
   218	            keccak256(payloads[3]),
   219	            keccak256(abi.encodeCall(IStakingSlasherAuth.setAuthorizedSlasher, (oldAggregator, false))),
   220	            "step (4) revokes the PREDECESSOR, not the aggregator step (3) just armed"
   221	        );
   222	        // CC-48 round-8 LOW-5: capture the implementation the proxy points at BEFORE the
   223	        // batch. `assertEq(registry.version(), "Registry-5.8.0")` after execution is a
   224	        // VACUOUS assertion -- `UUPSDeployHelper` already deployed 5.8.0, so it held before
   225	        // the batch too. The implementation SLOT moving is the non-vacuous statement that
   226	        // step (1) actually executed.
   227	        bytes32 implSlot = 0x360894a13ba1a3210667c828492db98dca3e2076cc3735a920a3ca505d382bbc;
   228	        address implBefore = address(uint160(uint256(vm.load(address(registry), implSlot))));
   229	
   230	        // ---- schedule ----
   231	        vm.prank(stranger);
   232	        vm.expectRevert(); // AccessControlUnauthorizedAccount: only the proposer may schedule
   233	        timelock.scheduleBatch(targets, values, payloads, NO_PREDECESSOR, BATCH_SALT, MIN_DELAY);
   234	
   235	        vm.prank(proposer);
   236	        timelock.scheduleBatch(targets, values, payloads, NO_PREDECESSOR, BATCH_SALT, MIN_DELAY);
   237	
   238	        bytes32 opId = timelock.hashOperationBatch(targets, values, payloads, NO_PREDECESSOR, BATCH_SALT);
   239	        assertTrue(timelock.isOperationPending(opId), "scheduled");
   240	        assertFalse(timelock.isOperationReady(opId), "but not ready yet");
   241	
   242	        // ---- the delay is real ----
   243	        vm.prank(executor);
   244	        vm.expectRevert();
   245	        timelock.executeBatch(targets, values, payloads, NO_PREDECESSOR, BATCH_SALT);
   246	
   247	        // Nothing has moved: this is the window, and it is not executable.
   248	        assertEq(keccak256(bytes(registry.version())), keccak256("Registry-5.10.1"));
   249	        assertEq(registry.maxTotalCreditExposure(), 0, "caps still unset while pending");
   250	        assertEq(registry.blsAggregator(), address(0), "aggregator still unwired while pending");
   251	
   252	        vm.warp(block.timestamp + MIN_DELAY);
   253	        assertTrue(timelock.isOperationReady(opId), "ready after the delay, not before");
   254	
   255	        // ---- THE atomicity property ----
   256	        // A Timelock operation id commits to the WHOLE (targets, values, payloads,
   257	        // predecessor, salt) tuple. Any proper subset — in particular "just the upgrade",
   258	        // which is the step that leaves the cap slot at 0 — hashes to a DIFFERENT id that
   259	        // was never scheduled, so it cannot be executed first. There is no ordering an
   260	        // executor can choose that produces the intermediate state.
   261	        (address[] memory upgradeOnlyT, uint256[] memory upgradeOnlyV, bytes[] memory upgradeOnlyP) =
   262	            _upgradeOnlySubBatch(targets, values, payloads);
   263	        bytes32 subId =
   264	            timelock.hashOperationBatch(upgradeOnlyT, upgradeOnlyV, upgradeOnlyP, NO_PREDECESSOR, BATCH_SALT);
   265	        assertTrue(subId != opId, "a subset is a different operation");
   266	        assertFalse(timelock.isOperation(subId), "and it was never scheduled");
   267	        vm.prank(executor);
   268	        vm.expectRevert();
   269	        timelock.executeBatch(upgradeOnlyT, upgradeOnlyV, upgradeOnlyP, NO_PREDECESSOR, BATCH_SALT);
   270	
   271	        // Still untouched after the failed slicing attempt.
   272	        assertEq(registry.maxTotalCreditExposure(), 0);
   273	
   274	        // ---- execute: one transaction, every effect ----
   275	        vm.prank(executor);
   276	        timelock.executeBatch(targets, values, payloads, NO_PREDECESSOR, BATCH_SALT);
   277	
   278	        assertTrue(timelock.isOperationDone(opId), "executed");
   279	        address implAfter = address(uint160(uint256(vm.load(address(registry), implSlot))));
   280	        assertTrue(implAfter != implBefore, "step (1) actually re-pointed the proxy");

hook: PostToolUse

    61

    63	
    64	## **关键结论（按严重级别）**
    65	
    66	### 1) **Critical：Blacklist 更新可绕过“DVT 共识”假设**
    67	位置：Registry 的 `updateOperatorBlacklist`（见 [Registry.sol:L525-L544](file:///Users/jason/Dev/mycelium/my-exploration/projects/SuperPaymaster/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol#L525-L544)）
    68	
    69	现状：
    70	- 该函数注释写的是“via DVT consensus”，但实现是：
    71	  - 只要 `msg.sender` 在 `isReputationSource` 白名单里，就能更新黑名单。
    72	  - BLS 校验是“可选的”：只有在 `blsValidator != 0` 且 `proof.length > 0` 才验证；传空 proof 直接跳过验证。
    73	
    74	攻击/风险：
    75	- 一旦某个 reputation source 私钥/合约被攻破，攻击者可以 **不提供 proof** 批量封禁/解封用户，造成大规模拒绝服务、业务风控失效。
    76	- 即使你们配置了 BLSValidator，也挡不住“传空 proof”的绕过路径。
    77	
    78	建议（强烈）：
    79	- 将 `updateOperatorBlacklist` 的 BLS proof 变为 **强制**（不允许空 proof），并做 **消息绑定**（domain separator + chainid + action type + 参数）以防跨链/跨场景重放。
    80	- 同时建议给这类治理动作增加 `proposalId/nonce` 的重放保护（你们在 `batchUpdateGlobalReputation` 已经部分做了）。
    81	
    82	---
    83	
    84	### 2) **High：Role 治理权限数据源不一致（易误配/锁死治理）**
    85	位置：Registry 的 `configureRole / setRoleLockDuration / createNewRole / setRoleOwner`（见 [Registry.sol:L357-L426](file:///Users/jason/Dev/mycelium/my-exploration/projects/SuperPaymaster/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol#L357-L426)）
    86	
    87	现状：
    88	- `IRegistry.RoleConfig` 里有 `owner` 字段（见 [IRegistry.sol:L31-L48](file:///Users/jason/Dev/mycelium/my-exploration/projects/SuperPaymaster/contracts/src/interfaces/v3/IRegistry.sol#L31-L48)），但实现里权限校验使用的是 `roleOwners[roleId]` 这个独立 mapping。
    89	- `_initRole` 初始化时把 owner 填进了 `RoleConfig.owner`，但 **没有同步写入 `roleOwners`**（导致默认 `roleOwners[roleId] == 0`）。
    90	- 结果就是：除非后续显式调用 `setRoleOwner`，否则这些 role 的“owner”形同虚设，治理权可能被意外锁死在 `Ownable.owner()` 手里。
    91	
    92	风险：
    93	- 这不是直接“被黑”，但会非常容易出现治理误操作：你以为 role owner 能调参，实际上不行；或者你以为以 `RoleConfig.owner` 为准，实际上以 `roleOwners` 为准。
    94	- 一旦治理流程依赖“role owner 分权”，当前实现会变成单点权限或无法治理。
    95	
    96	建议：
    97	- 统一“单一事实来源”：要么彻底只用 `RoleConfig.owner`，要么确保所有路径都同步更新 `roleOwners` 且初始化时写入（并给 `setRoleOwner` 发事件）。
    98	- 做一次部署后巡检：把所有关键 role 的 owner 读出来核对（防止主网已处于“看似有 owner 实则为 0”的状态）。
    99	
   100	---
   101	
   102	### 3) **Medium：Community ENS 未做唯一性约束，易被钓鱼/混淆**
   103	位置：`_validateAndExtractStake` 对 `ROLE_COMMUNITY` 的校验（见 [Registry.sol:L620-L636](file:///Users/jason/Dev/mycelium/my-exploration/projects/SuperPaymaster/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol#L620-L636)）
   104	
   105	现状：
   106	- 只检查 `name` 唯一（`communityByName`），但 `ensName` 仅在写入时更新 `communityByENS`，注册时不阻止重复。
   107	- 可能出现多个 community 声称同一个 ENS（或大小写/空白变体），前端/索引层很容易被欺骗。
   108	
   109	建议：
   110	- 为 ENS 增加唯一性校验（并建议做规范化：lowercase、trim、punycode 等）。
   111	
   112	---
   113	
   114	### 4) **~~Medium：accountToUser 可被任意覆盖（潜在身份绑定风险）~~ — RESOLVED**
   115	
   116	**处置（feat/remove-account-to-user）**：整个 `accountToUser` mapping 与
   117	`EndUserRoleData.account` 字段已被 **彻底删除**。链上没有任何消费方
   118	（SDK 里 `user == account == AA address`；`SuperPaymaster.isEligibleForSponsorship`
   119	用的是 `sbtHolders[user]` 而非 `accountToUser`），所以选择删除而非加固 EIP-712
   120	授权路径。此条 finding 不再适用。
   121	
   122	---
   123	
   124	### 5) **Medium：safeMintForRole 维护的角色枚举结构不一致**
   125	位置：`safeMintForRole`（见 [Registry.sol:L321-L355](file:///Users/jason/Dev/mycelium/my-exploration/projects/SuperPaymaster/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol#L321-L355)）
   126	
   127	现状：
   128	- `registerRole` 会维护 `userRoles[user].push(roleId)`，而 `safeMintForRole` 只增加 `userRoleCount`，不更新 `userRoles`。
   129	- 结果：`getUserRoles` 返回值可能不完整，进而影响前端/索引/审计工具对用户权限的判断。
   130	
   131	建议：
   132	- 统一维护口径：所有授予路径都更新 `userRoles`（并去重），或干脆删除 `userRoles` 只用事件索引。
   133	
   134	---
   135	
   136	## **ReputationSystem 合约专项（设计/可用性 + DoS 面）**
   137	位置：[ReputationSystem.sol](file:///Users/jason/Dev/mycelium/my-exploration/projects/SuperPaymaster/contracts/src/modules/reputation/ReputationSystem.sol)
   138	
   139	主要风险点：
   140	- **无上限数组导致 gas DoS 风险**：`boostedCollections`、`communityActiveRules` 都是只增不减（见 [ReputationSystem.sol:L26-L44](file:///Users/jason/Dev/mycelium/my-exploration/projects/SuperPaymaster/contracts/src/modules/reputation/ReputationSystem.sol#L26-L44)），`computeScore`/`getReputationBreakdown` 会遍历它们（见 [ReputationSystem.sol:L110-L155](file:///Users/jason/Dev/mycelium/my-exploration/projects/SuperPaymaster/contracts/src/modules/reputation/ReputationSystem.sol#L110-L155)、[ReputationSystem.sol:L201-L233](file:///Users/jason/Dev/mycelium/my-exploration/projects/SuperPaymaster/contracts/src/modules/reputation/ReputationSystem.sol#L201-L233)）。长期运行会把“计算/同步”变得越来越贵甚至不可用。
   141	- **规则集合可膨胀且缺少禁用/清理机制**：`setRule` 只在首次 base>0 时 push ruleId（见 [ReputationSystem.sol:L84-L96](file:///Users/jason/Dev/mycelium/my-exploration/projects/SuperPaymaster/contracts/src/modules/reputation/ReputationSystem.sol#L84-L96)），但没有 remove/disable 的完整闭环。
   142	- **输入结构缺少一致性校验**：`computeScore` 假设 `communities/ruleIds/activities` 维度对齐（见 [ReputationSystem.sol:L110-L139](file:///Users/jason/Dev/mycelium/my-exploration/projects/SuperPaymaster/contracts/src/modules/reputation/ReputationSystem.sol#L110-L139)）。如果未来有链上调用者传错维度会直接 revert（不是“被黑”，但会制造不稳定）。
   143	
   144	建议：
   145	- 对集合规模做上限（例如 boostedCollections 最大 N），并提供 remove/disable。
   146	- 对 computeScore 做维度校验（长度必须一致），提升可用性与可诊断性。
   147	
   148	---
   149	
   150	## **与你们业务强相关的一点：信用额度依赖 Registry 的声誉**
   151	SuperPaymaster 的 `getAvailableCredit` 直接使用 `REGISTRY.getCreditLimit(user)`（见 [SuperPaymaster.sol:L463-L487](file:///Users/jason/Dev/mycelium/my-exploration/projects/SuperPaymaster/contracts/src/paymasters/superpaymaster/v3/SuperPaymaster.sol#L463-L487)），因此：
   152	- `batchUpdateGlobalReputation` 的访问控制与 BLS 绑定是“钱的安全边界”之一（你们这里总体做得比黑名单路径严谨：见 [Registry.sol:L438-L519](file:///Users/jason/Dev/mycelium/my-exploration/projects/SuperPaymaster/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol#L438-L519)）。
   153	- 反过来，任何能绕过/误配声誉更新的点，都可能最终影响用户可用 credit 与坏账风险。
   154	
   155	---
   156	
   157	## **建议你立刻做的“最小代价核查清单”（不改代码也能做）**
   158	- 检查链上：`blsValidator` 是否已配置、`updateOperatorBlacklist` 的调用方是否严格走 BLS 流程（否则等价于“单点黑名单管理员”）。
   159	- 检查链上：每个关键 role 的 `roleOwners[roleId]` 是否为期望值（否则 role 分权不生效）。
   160	- 检查链上：是否已经存在重复 `ensName` 的 community（如果有，前端展示与路由要特别小心）。
   161	
   162	如果你愿意，我可以按“审计报告格式”（Threat model / Invariants / Findings / Recommendations / PoC 思路）再给你输出一版更正式、更适合给外部审计或基金会看的版本。
   585	#### H-7：`xPNTsToken.burnFromWithOpHash` 完全绕过每日 burn 限额（重新部署修）
   586	
   587	**文件**: `tokens/xPNTsToken.sol:408-435`
   588	
   589	**问题**: SuperPaymaster 走 `burnFromWithOpHash` 路径绕过每日 burn 限额（代码注释明确承认）。若 SP 被攻陷，攻击者可在紧急停止触发前通过多次调用（每次最多 `maxSingleTxLimit = 5000 ether`）清空所有持有者余额。
   590	
   591	**修复**: 对 `burnFromWithOpHash` 路径也施加每日累计总限额（建议 = `maxSingleTxLimit × 100`），保留快速燃烧能力的同时限制单日最大损失。

   599	| **M-A** | `BLSAggregator.sol:334` + `Registry.sol:391` | BLS slash 和 reputation 联合提案签名域不兼容（字段不一致），联合操作必然 revert | 重新部署：统一消息域或分拆为独立提案 |
   600	| **M-B** | `Registry.sol:207` | `setBLSAggregator`（Registry 侧）无时间锁；SuperPaymaster 侧有 24h 队列，Registry 侧没有，造成不对称 | UUPS：为 Registry.setBLSAggregator 加 24h 队列机制 |
   601	| **M-C** | `GTokenAuthorization.sol:113` | `setMySBT` 若设错地址（无 code 的 EOA），`balanceOf` 调用 revert，EIP-3009 永久失效且无法修复 | 重新部署时在 `setMySBT` 加 `mySBT_.code.length > 0` 校验 |
   602	| **M-D** | `PaymasterFactory.sol:197` | `cloneDeterministic(salt)` 不含 operator 命名空间，其他 operator 可抢占预计算地址（已在 L-1 记录，升级为 Medium）| 重新部署：`bytes32 actualSalt = keccak256(abi.encode(msg.sender, salt))` |
   603	| **M-E** | `Registry.sol:168-205` | `setStaking/setMySBT/setSuperPaymaster` 无时间锁，owner 泄露可立即替换为恶意合约 | UUPS：加 24-48h 时间锁队列（与已有 BLS timelock 保持一致） |
   604	| **M-F** | `GTokenStaking.sol:193` | `topUpStake` 的 `lock.amount += uint128(stakeAmount)` 缺少 uint128 溢出检查（其他函数有，此处漏了）| 重新部署：加 `if (stakeAmount > type(uint128).max) revert AmountExceedsUint128()` |
   605	| **M-G** | `MySBT.sol:264,328` | `membershipIndex` 默认值 0 在 swap-and-pop 后可能指向错误槽位，导致用户无法重新加入已离开的社区 | 重新部署：改用 1-indexed（0 = 未初始化），与 Registry.roleMemberIndex 保持一致 |
   606	| **M-H** | `Registry.sol:348` | `configureRole` 允许 roleOwner 将 `minStake` 设为 0，使高权限角色无需质押即可注册 | UUPS：对每个 roleId 设置 `minStake` 下限，或将 minStake 变更单独加时间锁 |

   609	
   610	### 8.4 Low / Info — 关键提示
   611	
   612	| ID | 文件 | 问题 | 建议 |
   613	|----|------|------|------|
   614	| **L-A** | `Registry.sol` | 当前 **24,352 字节**，距 EIP-170 上限仅 **224 字节**。任何 UUPS 升级的功能新增都可能超限 | 主网上线前必须先压缩 Registry ≥150B（这是 H-4 修复和 M-2 历史遗留修复的前提）|
   615	| **L-B** | `SuperPaymaster.sol:1588` | `__gap` 注释写 "21 used" 实际 34 个槽，严重误导未来升级评估 | UUPS：更新注释为 `// Slots 0-33 used (34 total). __gap[31] = 31 slots remaining.` |
   616	| **L-C** | `Registry.sol:399` | `batchUpdateGlobalReputation` 中 `epoch=0` 的提案被静默跳过，proposalId 已标记已执行，无法重试 | UUPS：入口加 `if (epoch == 0) revert InvalidParam()` |
   617	| **L-D** | `xPNTsFactory.sol:282` | `updatePrediction` 任何地址均可调用，可污染社区 prediction 数据 | 重新部署：限制为 COMMUNITY 角色调用 |
   618	| **L-E** | `DVTValidator.sol:258` | `setBLSAggregator` 无零地址检查，设为 `address(0)` 后 `markProposalExecuted` 永久 revert | 重新部署：加 `if (_bls == address(0)) revert InvalidAddress()` |
   619	| **L-F** | `SuperPaymaster.sol:766` | `withdraw` 不检查 `isConfigured`，任意地址可通过 `depositFor` 充值再提款，绕过角色检查 | UUPS：加 `require(operators[msg.sender].isConfigured)` |
   620	
    55	*   **分析:** 
    56	    *   代码逻辑允许 `ROLE_ENDUSER` 重复调用 `registerRole`。
    57	    *   **Stake:** 首次注册时锁定 Stake；后续注册（切换社区）时**跳过** `lockStake`。这是正确的设计，意味着 Stake 是“账号级”的通行证，而非“社区级”的押金。
    58	    *   **Metadata:** 每次注册都会更新 `roleMetadata`（指向最新的社区）。
    59	    *   **SBT:** 每次都会调用 `MySBT.mintForRole`，MySBT 内部逻辑正确处理了“添加新成员资格”而非重复铸造 Token。
    60	*   **结论:** ✅ **逻辑正确且安全**。这种设计允许用户低成本切换社区，同时保持全网唯一的质押门槛。
    61	
    62	### 4.2 Paymaster 注册的双重质押
    63	*   **分析:** 
    64	    *   注册 Paymaster 角色 (`ROLE_PAYMASTER_...`) 时，强制要求用户必须先拥有 `ROLE_COMMUNITY`。
    65	    *   这意味着一个 Paymaster 需要支付两份质押金：一份作为 Community（30 ETH），一份作为 Paymaster（30/50 ETH）。
    66	*   **结论:** ✅ **设计符合预期**（Skin in the game），增加了恶意节点的作恶成本。
    67	
    68	### 4.3 角色配置安全性
    69	*   **问题:** `configureRole` 函数允许 Role Owner 直接传入整个 `RoleConfig` 结构体覆盖配置。
    70	*   **风险:** 如果 Owner 操作失误，传入了 `minStake = 0` 或 `exitFeePercent = 10000` (100%)，可能导致严重的经济模型崩溃或用户资金被锁死。
    71	*   **建议:** 在 `configureRole` 中增加基本的参数健全性检查（Sanity Checks），例如 `require(config.minStake > 0)`，`require(config.exitFeePercent <= 2000)` 等。
    72	
    73	---
    74	
    75	## 5. 性能与 Gas 优化 (Performance)
    76	
    77	### 5.1 存储读取优化
    78	*   **现状:** `Registry` 在 `_validateAndExtractStake` 中多次读取 `roleConfigs[roleId].minStake`。
    79	*   **优化:** 整体流程中 `RoleConfig` 结构体被多次读取。虽然 Solidity 编译器做了一定优化，但在 `registerRole` 这种高频操作中，建议将 `RoleConfig` 缓存到内存中处理，减少 `SLOAD`。
    80	
    81	### 5.2 历史记录膨胀
    82	*   **现状:** `burnHistory` 和 `userBurnHistory` 是只会增长的数组。
    25

    28	The matrix distinguishes **contract gates** from the proposed governance deployment. `onlyOwner` checks an address; it does not itself enforce 48 hours. The governance plan calls for a Safe controlling a TimelockController with 172,800-second minimum delay and restricted roles; that configuration must be verified on each target chain. SP two-step transfer and the pending-owner upgrade stop are enforced in `contracts/src/utils/Ownable2StepNamespaced.sol:51`, `contracts/src/utils/Ownable2StepNamespaced.sol:64`, `contracts/src/utils/Ownable2StepNamespaced.sol:70` and `contracts/src/paymasters/superpaymaster/v3/BasePaymasterUpgradeable.sol:45`. Token community ownership instead transfers immediately in one call (`contracts/src/tokens/v2/xPNTsTokenV2Ext.sol:426`–`contracts/src/tokens/v2/xPNTsTokenV2Ext.sol:429`).

756:(`OperatorWithdrawalRequested` carries `readyAt` and `expiresAt`), so the exposure is limited to
   760	community-token issuance ceiling. Because escrow leaves `aPNTsBalance` at request time, the backing
   761	(and so the ceiling) drops when a withdrawal is requested, not when it executes. That is the

   766	into `aPNTsBalance` (escrow cancel/expire, postOp refund, stale release) therefore cannot overflow.

   772	## Reviewed redesign evidence (2026-11-13)
   773	
   774	The earlier sizes, source hashes, generated-bundle statements and whole-tree test
   775	counts above are historical and superseded by `.rv-redesign.md`. Current versions
   776	are BLSAggregator-4.14.0, DVTValidator-0.8.0 and Reputation-0.6.0. Genuine crypto
   777	tests, including the M1 replay regression, intentionally skip on Cancun; Prague
   778	evidence must execute the mandatory real-precompile sentinel successfully. CC115
   779	tests using AnchoredOverIssueFraudProofVerifier are **local adapter** evidence,
   780	not evidence of compatibility with the unadapted pinned upstream verifier.
   781	The historical Python executor remains byte-identical to origin/main; rc.8
   782	routing belongs in another PR.
   783	
   784	### M1 execution floor and disclosed residuals (2026-11-13)
   785	
   786	`BLSAggregator.verifyAndExecute` rejects `proposalId < proposalFloor` with
   787	`InvalidProposalId` only when `msg.sender == DVT_VALIDATOR`. M1 covers slash and
   788	aggregator-routed reputation proposals executed through the current DVT, where
   789	IDs are DVT-allocated. An allocated id with a ready proof becomes stale on that
   790	path when a later rotation raises the floor; reactivating its original DVT does
   791	not restore it. A newly allocated id at or above the fresh floor remains
   792	executable with its own valid proof. Every caller, including the owner, still
   793	receives `InvalidProposalId` for zero or an id at or above `2^128`. The monotonic
   794	floor preserves the exhausted value `2^128` across rotations.
   795	
   796	The accepted, disclosed residuals are:
   797	
   798	- Owner-direct `verifyAndExecute` accepts caller-chosen ids below the floor.
   799	  This is the documented owner-mediated residual: rotation does not revoke that
   800	  route's unused signed authorizations. Signature, replay and DVT callback checks
   801	  still apply; the floor must not treat an owner-chosen id as a DVT allocation.
   802	- Unused pre-rotation generic proposals remain owner/DVT-executable through
   803	  `executeProposal`; their typed replay keys are separate from the raw-id floor.
   804	- Pre-rotation reputation proofs can still reach Registry through an authorized
   805	  reputation source. That route remains source-gated, enforces per-user epoch
   806	  progression and bounds score changes to ±100. It does not inherit the
   807	  aggregator's execution floor.
   808	- An unused pre-rotation consensus queue proof can still set a reversible
   809	  pre-flag. A BLS-queued freeze has **no time expiry**:
   810	  `SuperPaymasterStorage._isSlashPending` expires only a guardian queue after
   811	  `GUARDIAN_SLASH_FREEZE`. The owner can always cancel the flag with
   812	  `SuperPaymasterAdmin.cancelSlash`. `usedSlashQueueHashes` makes the proof
  1000	    function verifyAndExecute(
  1001	        uint256 proposalId,
  1002	        address operator,
  1003	        uint8 slashLevel,
  1004	        address[] calldata repUsers,
  1005	        uint256[] calldata newScores,
  1006	        uint256 epoch,
  1007	        bytes32 evidenceHash,
  1008	        bytes calldata proof
  1009	    ) external nonReentrant {
  1010	        if (msg.sender != DVT_VALIDATOR && msg.sender != owner()) {
  1011	            revert UnauthorizedCaller(msg.sender);
  1012	        }
  1013	        // Raw ids (slash AND reputation) live below 2^128, providing 128-bit
  1014	        // cryptographic separation from generic and queue keccak keys. Only the DVT
  1015	        // path uses DVT-allocated ids; owner-direct ids are caller-chosen.
  1016	        if (proposalId == 0 || proposalId >> 128 != 0 || (msg.sender == DVT_VALIDATOR && proposalId < proposalFloor)) {
  1017	            revert InvalidProposalId();
  1018	        }
  1019	        if (executedProposals[proposalId]) {
  1020	            revert ProposalAlreadyExecuted(proposalId);
  1021	        }
  1022	        // CC-48 round-2: reject the combined shape before any signature work. See
  1023	        // CombinedProposalNotSupported — the aggregator and Registry pre-images for a
  1024	        // reputation batch can only agree when operator/slashLevel are absent, so a
  1025	        // reputation proposal carrying a slash target was never executable anyway
  1026	        // (it reverted deep inside Registry with an opaque BLSFailed). Failing here,
  1027	        // by name, turns a silent dead path into an explicit protocol rule.
  1028	        if (repUsers.length != 0 && (operator != address(0) || slashLevel != 0)) {
  1029	            revert CombinedProposalNotSupported();
  1030	        }
  1031	        // CC-48 round-3 LOW: pin the slash-only shape from the OTHER side too. The
  1032	        // slash pre-image commits to (proposalId, operator, slashLevel, epoch,
  1033	        // evidenceHash) and NOT to `newScores`, so a non-empty `newScores` here would be
  1034	        // caller-controlled input that passed a signature check without being covered by
  1035	        // any signature. It is unused on this branch today; requiring it empty means it
  1036	        // can never become used-but-unsigned by a later edit.
  1037	        if (repUsers.length == 0) {
  1038	            if (newScores.length != 0) revert CombinedProposalNotSupported();
  1039	            // A proposal with no batch, no target and no severity only burns a
  1040	            // proposalId in this contract and in Registry. It needs a real quorum, so it
  1041	            // is not an attack surface — but a no-op shape on a consensus path is a
  1042	            // place for future divergence, and CC-48 is about making the protocol rules
  1043	            // explicit rather than incidental.
  1044	            if (operator == address(0) && slashLevel == 0) revert EmptyProposalNotSupported();
  1045	        }
  1046	
  1047	        // 1. Construct expected message + resolve the required threshold.
  1048	        // The signed message MUST commit to chainid to prevent cross-chain replay.
  1160	        _checkSignatures(proof, expectedMessageHash, requiredThreshold);
  1161	        uint256 signerMask = abi.decode(proof[:32], (uint256));
  1162	        _commitSigners(replayKey, signerMask, expectedMessageHash);
  1163	
  1164	        // 4. Execute Call
  1165	        (bool success, bytes memory returnData) = target.call(callData);
  1166	        if (!success) revert ProposalExecutionFailed(proposalId, returnData);
  1167	
  1168	        // 5. Mark as Executed
  1169	        executedProposals[replayKey] = true;
  1170	
  1171	        emit ProposalExecuted(proposalId, target, keccak256(callData));
  1172	    }
  1173	
  1174	    // ====================================
  1175	    // Internal Functions
  1176	    // ====================================
  1177	
  1178	    /// @dev Validate that a G1 point is:
  1179	    ///      1. Not the identity (point at infinity — all-zero coordinates).
  1180	    ///      2. On the BLS12-381 G1 curve — verified via G1ADD precompile (0x0b)
  1181	    ///         by adding the point to the identity. The precompile rejects points
  1182	    ///         not on the curve with a failed staticcall.
  1183	    ///      3. In the prime-order subgroup of G1 — verified via G1MUL precompile
  1184	    ///         (0x0c) by multiplying by the subgroup order r. A point in the
  1185	    ///         main subgroup must satisfy r*P = O (identity). Points in small
  1186	    ///         subgroups would produce a non-zero result.
  1187	    ///
  1188	    ///      EIP-2537 G1ADD input:  256 bytes (two 128-byte G1 points, big-endian).

## Confirmations of known/disclosed items

### K-1 — Medium — Role owners can rewrite admission and exit policy

- **Location:** [Registry.sol:497](/Users/jason/Dev/aastar/sp-wt/rc8-bls/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol:497), enforcement at [Registry.sol:333](/Users/jason/Dev/aastar/sp-wt/rc8-bls/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol:333) and [Registry.sol:424](/Users/jason/Dev/aastar/sp-wt/rc8-bls/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol:424).
- **Precondition:** Control of the Registry owner or the applicable `RoleConfig.owner`.
- **Call sequence:** Read the role row → call `configureRole(roleId, config)` with lowered `minStake`/`roleLockDuration`, or an extremely large lock duration → new users register under the weakened policy, or existing users call `exitRole`.
- **Impact:** A privileged caller can remove stake requirements, change fees and activity state, transfer role ownership, shorten locks, or extend existing members’ locks. A near-maximum duration can also make `lockedAt + lockDuration` overflow and prevent exit until governance corrects the configuration.
- **5.5.0 reachability:** **Yes**, through the known deployer-held Registry/role-owner privileges. This is the previously disclosed broad `configureRole` authority, not a new finding.
- **Minimal fix:** Give sensitive fields separate bounded setters; snapshot exit deadlines per lock instead of consulting mutable policy at exit; route admission/lock changes through the intended timelock.

### K-2 — Low — Pre-rotation reputation authorizations remain valid on the direct source route

- **Location:** [Registry.sol:574](/Users/jason/Dev/aastar/sp-wt/rc8-bls/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol:574), domain construction at [Registry.sol:547](/Users/jason/Dev/aastar/sp-wt/rc8-bls/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol:547).
- **Precondition:** A valid, unused reputation proof was signed before a DVT rotation; the caller can invoke an authorized source such as `ReputationSystem`.
- **Call sequence:** Rotate DVT without changing the retained aggregator/Registry domain → call `ReputationSystem.syncToRegistry(...)` with the old matching proof → it calls `batchUpdateGlobalReputation`.
- **Impact:** The old authorization can still apply a bounded ±100 reputation change and corresponding credit-tier movement despite the aggregator-routed proposal floor.
- **5.5.0 reachability:** **Yes**. Explicitly disclosed in `rc8-daybreak-fixes.md`; source gating, epoch progression, proposal replay protection, and credit caps still apply.
- **Minimal fix:** Add a Registry-side reputation generation/floor to the signed preimage and increment it on DVT rotation.

### K-3 — Low — Next-nonce blacklist proof survives DVT rotation

- **Location:** [Registry.sol:663](/Users/jason/Dev/aastar/sp-wt/rc8-bls/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol:663), nonce binding at [Registry.sol:676](/Users/jason/Dev/aastar/sp-wt/rc8-bls/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol:676).
- **Precondition:** An unused valid proof exists for `blacklistNonce + 1`, and the retained aggregator executes the call after rotation.
- **Call sequence:** Sign the next blacklist action → rotate DVT without changing the Registry BLS domain → aggregator invokes `updateOperatorBlacklist` → Registry increments the nonce, verifies the old proof, and forwards the statuses.
- **Impact:** Users can be blocked or unblocked using a pre-rotation authorization. No funds move.
- **5.5.0 reachability:** **Yes**. This is the explicitly disclosed blacklist residual.
- **Minimal fix:** Bind a rotation generation to blacklist proofs, or invalidate the blacklist nonce/domain during rotation.

### K-4 — Info — Credit exposure is exact only for the governance-declared population

- **Location:** [Registry.sol:832](/Users/jason/Dev/aastar/sp-wt/rc8-bls/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol:832), accounting at [Registry.sol:617](/Users/jason/Dev/aastar/sp-wt/rc8-bls/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol:617) and [Registry.sol:654](/Users/jason/Dev/aastar/sp-wt/rc8-bls/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol:654).
- **Precondition:** Governance invalidates/reseeds the population but omits an existing above-floor user and supplies an `expectedPopulationTotal` matching the incomplete list.
- **Call sequence:** Change an active credit tier or threshold → population becomes unseeded → call `seedCreditPopulation(incompleteUsers, incompleteCount, true)` → future reputation proposals resume.
- **Impact:** `totalCreditExposure` understates outstanding above-floor credit until an omitted user is touched by a later proposal and backfilled. This is documented directly in the contract and remains an operator-roster trust assumption.
- **5.5.0 reachability:** **Yes**, but only through owner-controlled seeding; the ordinary proposal delta arithmetic and backfill logic were internally consistent.
- **Minimal fix:** Maintain an enumerable reputation population from inception. For legacy migration, bind finalization to a reviewed manifest/Merkle commitment rather than a count supplied by the same caller.

## Controls verified

- Initialization atomically grants Registry ownership, all seven `RoleConfig.owner` values, and temporary reputation-source status to `_owner`. `DeployLive` subsequently authorizes exactly ReputationSystem and BLSAggregator and revokes the deployer source at [DeployLive.s.sol:430](/Users/jason/Dev/aastar/sp-wt/rc8-bls/contracts/script/v3/DeployLive.s.sol:430). The proxy is constructed with initialization calldata, so the deployment path exposes no separate initialization transaction.
- `markProposalExecuted` is aggregator-only at [Registry.sol:693](/Users/jason/Dev/aastar/sp-wt/rc8-bls/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol:693). Generic BLS execution denies that selector. Repeated marking is idempotent; arbitrary users cannot consume IDs.
- `Ownable2StepNamespaced` uses the correctly derived ERC-7201 slot, clears nominations on every ownership change, disables renunciation, and `_authorizeUpgrade` requires both current ownership and no pending nominee at [Registry.sol:1012](/Users/jason/Dev/aastar/sp-wt/rc8-bls/contracts/src/core/Registry.sol:1012).
- The current Prague artifact’s Registry source hash exactly matches the audited source. Its 34-entry layout equals the pinned 32-entry deployed snapshot plus only `pendingSBTBurn@31` and `pendingMembershipCleanup@32`; the gap moves to slot 33 with 41 slots, preserving end slot 74. The layout comparator returned no differences, and its read-only self-test rejected all nine mutations.
- No files were modified. Forge compilation/tests were not rerun because this environment’s filesystem policy prevented build/cache writes; existing source-matched artifacts were used for the storage verification.

**Counts — NEW: Critical 0, High 0, Medium 0, Low 0, Info 0; confirmed known/disclosed: Medium 1, Low 2, Info 1.**

No new security findings were identified in target (b).
